by buying power want become?", "chosen": "When backed by buying power, \"want\" can become \"demand\". Here's why:\n\n1. **Want**: This is a subjective feeling or desire for something. It's personal and not necessarily backed by the ability or intention to purchase. For example, \"I want a million dollars\" doesn't mean you're actively trying to acquire it or have the means to do so.\n\n2. **Demand**: This is a want that is backed by buying power.
